- **survey-writing**: Taxonomy-focused literature review and survey paper writing. Given a research field or topic, searches comprehensively for papers, builds a multi-level taxonomy, and drafts a complete survey with introduction, background, per-category deep dives, comparison tables, and future directions. Unlike literature-survey, this skill focuses purely on organizing existing work — no cross-domain exploration or idea generation. Use when the user wants to "write a survey", "create a literature review", "organize papers into a taxonomy", or "draft a related work section".
